# Silent reasoning critic log

Rows are never edited; a correction is a new row. "Logged" is the minute the row was written (`date -u`); the commit time is what counts for pre-registration. Appended only by the critic, except the one row per registration or amendment in which the operator appends the commit SHA. Every row that rules on a number states what had been seen when it was written.

Direction, so nobody calls the larger sigma "conservative": a larger sigma loosens the bar leg (acc >= bar - sigma) and tightens the "ahead of the null by sigma" legs, K2's line and the K3 margin; it cuts both ways, which is exactly why it is applied as the text says and not chosen per line. **Labels under the ruling**: as the table prints them. The alternatives flip labels only (section 4): without the ProsQA term (1.95) the K3 "no better than plain depth" label would drop from pause, c1b_k8 and c1b_k4 and CURRICULUM-ONLY from c2 (+2.4) and c2_shuffled (+2.0); at the floor (1.0) also from coconut (+1.4), the C1b accuracy part would fail (-1.6 below -1.0) and c2_shuffled would read "fading at one 0.5 B reading". None of these is the registered reading; no advance, kill or K3 outcome moves under any of the three. The author's pending "one run" reading of the determinism pair (20:55Z) would raise sigma_task on ProsQA to 3.69; A3.4's term is the seed SD itself, not sigma_task, so sigma_screen stays 3.60 under either answer. ProsQA-hard's sigma_screen takes its own bar's p under the same max (its binomial term on n = 300 is about 2.7 at p near 0.65), with the same 3.60 third term. |
